# Unit.Main

<!-- documentation: model-written from source via the local LLM; review before trusting -->

# Unit

The `Unit` class is the central abstraction for any living entity in the world that can engage in combat, possess health and power resources, cast spells, and move. It serves as the base class for `Player`, `Creature`, `Pet`, and `Totem`. `Unit` manages the core mechanics of the game loop for these entities: updating health/power, processing spell auras, handling melee and spell damage calculations, managing threat and aggro, controlling movement splines, and enforcing visibility and stealth rules. It does not handle database persistence or specific AI behaviors (which reside in derived classes or attached AI objects), but rather provides the mechanical engine upon which those behaviors operate.

## Purpose & Responsibilities

*   **Combat Engine:** Implements the logic for melee swings, spell hits, damage calculation (including armor, resistances, absorbs, and criticals), and outcome determination (miss, dodge, parry, block, crush, glance).
*   **Resource Management:** Tracks and modifies Health, Mana, Rage, Energy, Focus, and Happiness. Handles regeneration timers and resource costs.
*   **Spell & Aura System:** Manages the application, stacking, removal, and periodic ticking of spell auras. Handles diminishing returns (DR) for crowd control effects.
*   **Threat & Aggro:** Maintains the threat list (`ThreatManager`) and hostile references (`HostileRefManager`) to determine target selection and evade modes for creatures.
*   **Movement Control:** Interfaces with `MotionMaster` and `MoveSpline` to control positioning, speeds, and movement flags (root, hover, water walking). Handles server-side movement validation and anti-cheat synchronization.
*   **State Management:** Tracks combat state, death state, stealth/invisibility, and various unit flags (PvP, mounted, shapeshifted).
*   **Relations:** Manages ownership, charming, possession, and follower relationships (pets, guardians, totems).

## Member-by-Member Behavior

### Construction and Lifecycle
*   **Unit**: Initializes the unit's internal structures, including the `MotionMaster`, `ThreatManager`, `HostileRefManager`, and `MoveSpline`. Sets default values for stats, resistances, and movement speeds.
*   **~Unit**: Cleans up resources. Marks current spells as deletable, deletes the `CharmInfo` structure if present, and deletes the `MoveSpline`. Asserts that spell-related game objects and dynamic objects have already been removed.
*   **Update**: The core tick function called periodically. It updates spell cooldowns, processes pending procs, handles mana regeneration delays, manages the combat timer (for PvP/leave-combat logic), executes extra attacks, updates melee attack timers, checks health-based aura states, and updates movement splines. It delegates heavy motion updates to async threads if configured.
*   **AddToWorld**: Registers the unit with the world, schedules an AI notification, and initializes combat and proc timers.
*   **RemoveFromWorld**: Cleans up relations (uncharms, removes guardians/pets/game objects), clears deleted auras, and removes the unit from the world grid.
*   **CleanupsBeforeDelete**: Final cleanup before destruction. Interrupts spells, kills events, stops combat, clears combo points and threat lists, and removes all auras.
*   **AddFollower / RemoveFollower**: Manages the list of units following this unit via `FollowerRefManager`.
*   **AddHatedBy / RemoveHatedBy**: Manages references to units that hate this unit via `HostileRefManager`.
*   **RelocationNotifyEvent**: An internal event class used to notify nearby entities of a unit's relocation, triggering aggro checks.
*   **Abort**: Cancels a scheduled AI notification event.
*   **ScheduleAINotify**: Schedules an AI notification event to occur after a delay, used to defer aggro checks after movement.
*   **IsAINotifyScheduled / SetAINotifyScheduled**: Checks or sets whether an AI notification is currently scheduled.
*   **IsLinkingEventTrigger**: Checks if the unit is flagged as a trigger for creature linking events.

### Stats and Resources
*   **SetCreateHealth / GetCreateHealth**: Sets/gets the base health value determined by level/class.
*   **SetCreateStat / GetCreateStat**: Sets/gets base primary stats (Strength, Agility, etc.).
*   **SetCreateMana / GetCreateMana**: Sets/gets base mana value.
*   **SetCreateResistance / GetCreateResistance**: Sets/gets base magical resistances.
*   **SetStat / GetStat**: Sets/gets the current effective stat values after modifiers.
*   **SetResistance / GetResistance**: Sets/gets current effective resistances.
*   **SetArmor / GetArmor**: Sets/gets armor (stored as Normal school resistance).
*   **SetHealth / GetHealth / ModifyHealth**: Manages current health. `ModifyHealth` clamps values between 0 and MaxHealth.
*   **SetMaxHealth / GetMaxHealth**: Manages maximum health capacity.
*   **SetPower / GetPower / ModifyPower**: Manages current power (Mana/Rage/Energy/etc.) for a specific power type.
*   **SetMaxPower / GetMaxPower**: Manages maximum power capacity.
*   **SetPowerType / GetPowerType**: Changes the active power resource type (e.g., from Mana to Rage).
*   **GetRegenHPPerSpirit / GetRegenMPPerSpirit**: Calculates health and mana regeneration rates based on Spirit and Class.
*   **UpdateStats / UpdateAllStats / UpdateResistances / UpdateArmor / UpdateMaxHealth / UpdateMaxPower / UpdateManaRegen / UpdateAttackPowerAndDamage / UpdateDamagePhysical**: Virtual declarations for derived classes to recalculate derived stats when base stats or modifiers change.
*   **GetCreatePowers**: Returns the base power value for a specific power type.
*   **ApplyPowerMod / ApplyMaxPowerMod**: Applies a flat modifier to current or maximum power.
*   **SetPowerPercent**: Sets power to a specific percentage of maximum.
*   **GetTotalStatValue / GetTotalResistanceValue / GetTotalAuraModValue**: Calculates total values for stats, resistances, or aura modifiers by combining base, percentage, and total modifiers.
*   **GetModifierValue**: Retrieves a specific modifier value from the aura modifier group.
*   **HandleStatModifier / HandleAttackPowerModifier**: Applies or removes a stat or attack power modifier, triggering stat updates if allowed.
*   **GetAttackPowerModifierValue**: Retrieves the current attack power modifier value.
*   **GetSpellSchoolByAuraGroup / GetStatByAuraGroup / GetPowerTypeByAuraGroup**: Maps aura modifier groups to their corresponding spell schools, stats, or power types.
*   **SetModifierValue**: Directly sets a modifier value in the aura modifier group.
*   **CanModifyStats / SetCanModifyStats**: Controls whether stat modifications are allowed (typically disabled during initialization).
*   **GetTotalAttackPowerValue**: Calculates total attack power including base and modifiers.
*   **GetWeaponDamageRange**: Retrieves the minimum or maximum damage range for a weapon attack type.
*   **GetWeaponDamageSchool / SetWeaponDamageSchool / GetWeaponDamageCount / SetBaseWeaponDamage**: Manages weapon damage properties.
*   **GetPPMProcChance**: Calculates the proc-per-minute chance for an item effect.
*   **UpdateCastSpeed**: Recalculates cast speed based on active auras.
*   **ApplyAttackTimePercentMod**: Applies a percentage modifier to attack speed.
*   **GetSpellCritFromIntellect**: Calculates spell critical chance derived from Intelligence.
*   **UpdateSpellCritChance / UpdateAllSpellCritChances**: Updates spell critical chance for a specific school or all schools.
*   **GetSpellCritPercent / SetSpellCritPercent**: Gets or sets the spell critical chance for a school.
*   **GetShieldBlockValue**: Declaration for getting shield block value (implemented in derived classes).
*   **GetUnitDodgeChance / GetUnitParryChance / GetUnitBlockChance / GetUnitCriticalChance**: Calculates defensive chances based on stats, skills, and auras.
*   **MeleeMissChanceCalc**: Calculates the chance to miss a melee attack based on skill difference and dual-wielding penalties.
*   **RollMeleeOutcomeAgainst / RollMeleeOutcomeAgainst#2**: Determines the result of a melee attack (Crit, Miss, Dodge, Parry, Block, Crush, Glance, Normal) based on skill differences and chances.
*   **CalculateDamage**: Calculates raw weapon damage for a specific attack type.
*   **CalculateMeleeDamage**: Computes raw melee damage, applying bonuses, armor reduction, and determining hit outcomes.
*   **DealMeleeDamage**: Applies the calculated melee damage to the victim, handling durability loss, triggering damage shields, and sending combat logs.
*   **AttackerStateUpdate**: The core function for executing a melee swing. Calculates damage, triggers procs, deals damage, and sends the `SMSG_ATTACKERSTATEUPDATE` packet.
*   **SendAttackStateUpdate / SendAttackStateUpdate#2**: Sends the attack state update packet to clients.
*   **SendMeleeAttackStart / SendMeleeAttackStop**: Sends packets indicating the start or stop of a melee attack.
*   **ResetAttackTimer / SetAttackTimer / GetAttackTimer**: Manages the cooldown timers for melee attacks.
*   **IsAttackReady**: Checks if an attack timer has expired.
*   **HaveOffhandWeapon**: Checks if the unit has an offhand weapon equipped.
*   **CanUseEquippedWeapon**: Checks if an equipped weapon can be used (e.g., not disarmed).
*   **CanBeDisarmed**: Declaration for checking if the unit can be disarmed.
*   **UpdateMeleeAttackingState**: Executes melee swings if timers are ready. Handles main-hand and off-hand timing, sends error messages to players if attacks fail, and triggers `AttackerStateUpdate`.
*   **DelayAutoAttacks**: Delays melee attack timers if an attack cannot be performed immediately.
*   **CanAutoAttackTarget**: Checks if a unit can melee attack a target (range, facing, alive, pacified).
*   **ResetExtraAttacks / AddExtraAttack / SetExtraAttaks / GetExtraAttacks / IsExtraAttacksLocked / ExtraAttacksLocked / AddExtraAttackOnUpdate**: Manages extra attacks granted by effects like Bloodthirst.
*   **ClearAllReactives / StartReactiveTimer / GetReactiveTarget / UpdateReactives**: Manages reactive abilities (like Berserking or Defense) that trigger on specific combat events.
*   **GetWeaponBasedAuraModifier**: Declaration for getting weapon-based aura modifiers.

### Combat and Damage
*   **DealDamage**: The universal damage dealer. Handles health modification, death checks, loot recipient assignment, threat addition, PvP flags, and spell interruptions. It distinguishes between direct damage, DoTs, and environmental damage.
*   **Kill**: Handles the death sequence. Determines loot recipients, rewards XP/Honor, triggers death auras (Spirit of Redemption), sets death state, interrupts spells, and notifies AI and battlegrounds.
*   **DoKillUnit**: Instantly kills a unit by dealing damage equal to its current health.
*   **CalculateDamageAbsorbAndResist**: Calculates how much damage is absorbed by shields and resisted by magical resistances. Handles school-specific absorbs, mana shields, and damage splitting (Blessing of Sacrifice, Soul Link).
*   **CalculateAbsorbResistBlock**: Calculates absorb, resist, and block values for spell damage.
*   **TriggerDamageShields**: Triggers reflective damage shields (Thorns, Flame Ward) on the victim.
*   **IsImmuneToDamage / IsImmuneToSpell / IsImmuneToSpellEffect / IsImmuneToSchool / IsImmuneToSchoolMask / IsImmuneToMechanic / IsTotalImmune**: Checks various immunity flags and auras to determine if a spell or damage type affects the unit.
*   **SpellDamageBonusTaken / SpellHealingBonusTaken**: Calculates modifiers to damage/healing taken by the unit.
*   **SpellBaseDamageBonusTaken / SpellBaseHealingBonusTaken**: Retrieves flat bonus values for damage/healing taken.
*   **MeleeDamageBonusTaken**: Calculates modifiers to melee damage taken.
*   **IsSpellCrit**: Determines if a spell hit is a critical strike.
*   **IsEffectResist**: Checks if a spell effect is resisted based on mechanic resistance.
*   **RollMagicResistanceMultiplierOutcomeAgainst**: Calculates the resistance multiplier for magic damage.
*   **IsSpellPartiallyBlocked / RollSpellBlockChanceOutcome**: Determines if a spell can be partially blocked and rolls the chance.
*   **ApplySpellImmune / ApplySpellDispelImmunity**: Applies or removes spell immunities.
*   **SendEnvironmentalDamageLog**: Sends a combat log entry for environmental damage.
*   **UnitDamaged**: Records damage taken from a specific source.

### Spells and Auras
*   **AddSpellAuraHolder / RemoveSpellAuraHolder**: Adds or removes a complete spell aura holder (containing all effects of a spell) from the unit's list. Handles stacking rules and visual slot limits.
*   **AddAura / RefreshAura**: Helper methods to apply or refresh specific spells as auras.
*   **HasAura / HasAuraType / GetAura / GetAurasByType**: Queries for the presence of specific spells or aura types. Note: `HasAuraType` is declared here but implemented in derived classes or other partials; this unit provides the interface.
*   **RemoveAurasDueToSpell / RemoveAurasByCasterSpell / RemoveAurasDueToSpellByCancel**: Removes auras based on spell ID, caster, or cancellation flags.
*   **RemoveSpellsCausingAura / RemoveSpellsCausingAura#2 / RemoveSpellsCausingAuraWithMechanic / RemoveNonPassiveSpellsCausingAura**: Removes all spells that cause a specific aura type, optionally filtering by mechanic or passivity.
*   **RemoveAllAuras / RemoveAllNegativeAuras / RemoveAllAurasOnDeath / RemoveAuraTypeOnDeath**: Bulk removal of auras, including specific handling for death.
*   **RemoveAura / RemoveAura#2 / RemoveSingleAuraFromSpellAuraHolder / RemoveSingleAuraFromSpellAuraHolder#2 / RemoveSingleAuraDueToItemSet**: Removes individual auras or specific effects from aura holders.
*   **RemoveAurasWithInterruptFlags / RemoveAurasWithAttribute / RemoveAurasWithDispelType / RemoveAurasAtMechanicImmunity / RemoveAurasDueToItemSpell / RemoveNotOwnSingleTargetAuras**: Removes auras based on interrupt flags, attributes, dispel types, mechanic immunity, item association, or ownership.
*   **RemoveAuraHolderFromStack / RemoveAuraHolderDueToSpellByDispel**: Removes auras from stacks or due to dispels.
*   **RemoveNoStackAurasDueToAuraHolder**: Removes conflicting auras when adding a new one, implementing complex stacking rules.
*   **RemoveAuraDueToVisibleSlotLimit**: Removes an aura if the visible slot limit is exceeded.
*   **GetVisibleAurasCount**: Counts the number of visible auras.
*   **HandleTriggers**: Processes aura procs (triggers) based on damage or spell events.
*   **ProcDamageAndSpellFor / ProcSkillsAndReactives**: Handles the logic for triggering procs and updating reactive abilities.
*   **GetDiminishing / IncrDiminishing / ApplyDiminishingToDuration / ApplyDiminishingAura / ClearDiminishings**: Manages Diminishing Returns (DR) for crowd control effects, reducing duration based on recent applications.
*   **IsUnderLastManaUseEffect / SetLastManaUse**: Checks if the unit is in the brief window after casting a mana-consuming spell where mana regeneration is paused.
*   **GetSpellAuraHolderMap / GetSpellAuraHolderMap#2 / GetSpellAuraHolder / GetSpellAuraHolder#2 / GetSpellAuraHolderBounds / GetSpellAuraHolderBounds#2**: Accessors for the map of spell aura holders.
*   **GetSingleCastSpellTargets / GetSingleCastSpellTargets#2**: Accessors for single-cast spell targets.
*   **AddAuraToModList**: Adds an aura to the modifier list for quick lookup.
*   **CleanupDeletedAuras**: Deletes auras that have been marked for deletion.
*   **DeleteAuraHolder**: Marks an aura holder for deletion.
*   **DelaySpellAuraHolder**: Delays the expiration of a spell aura holder.
*   **_RemoveAllAuraMods / _ApplyAllAuraMods**: Applies or removes all aura modifiers.
*   **UpdateAuraForGroup**: Updates aura information for group members.
*   **AddPetAura / RemovePetAura**: Manages pet-specific auras.
*   **GetAuraApplicationMask / GetNegativeAuraApplicationMask**: Generates masks representing applied auras.
*   **GetTotalAuraModifier / GetTotalAuraMultiplier / GetMaxPositiveAuraModifier / GetMaxNegativeAuraModifier / GetTotalAuraModifierByMiscMask / GetTotalAuraMultiplierByMiscMask / GetTotalAuraModifierByMiscValue / GetTotalAuraMultiplierByMiscValue**: Calculates total modifiers or multipliers for aura types, optionally filtered by misc mask or value.
*   **HasAuraTypeByCaster**: Checks for an aura type from a specific caster.
*   **HasAuraState**: Checks for a specific aura state flag.
*   **ModifyAuraState**: Applies or removes an aura state flag.
*   **HasStealthAura / HasInvisibilityAura / IsFeared / IsInRoots / IsFrozen / IsFeigningDeath / IsFeigningDeathSuccessfully**: Convenience methods for checking specific aura states.
*   **HasBreakableByDamageCrowdControlAura / HasBreakableByDamageAuraType / HasAuraPetShouldAvoidBreaking**: Checks for crowd control auras that can be broken by damage.
*   **HasMorePowerfulSpellActive**: Checks if a more powerful version of a spell is already active.
*   **GetMostImportantAuraAfter**: Finds the most important aura of a specific type.
*   **HandleNULLProc / HandleCantTrigger**: Default proc handlers that do nothing or indicate inability to trigger.
*   **GetSpellRank**: Gets the rank of a spell based on the unit's level.
*   **SendSpellGo / SendPlaySpellVisualKit / SendPeriodicAuraLog / WritePetSpellsCooldown / CancelSpellChannelingAnimationInstantly**: Sends spell-related packets to clients.
*   **AddGameObject / RemoveGameObject / RemoveGameObject#2 / RemoveAllGameObjects / GetGameObject**: Manages game objects created by spells.
*   **RemovedSpellData / operator< / operator==**: Helper struct and operators for tracking removed spells.
*   **CreateProcExtendMask**: Creates a mask for proc extensions.

### Movement
*   **GetMotionMaster / GetMotionMaster#2**: Accesses the `MotionMaster` object that controls the unit's movement generators.
*   **UpdateSpeed / SetSpeedRate / SetSpeedRateReal / GetSpeed / GetSpeedRate**: Manages movement speed rates for walk, run, swim, etc. Applies aura modifiers.
*   **SetRooted / SetRootedReal / IsRooted / ShouldBeRooted**: Sets or checks the rooted movement flag.
*   **SetWaterWalking / SetWaterWalkingReal / IsWaterWalking**: Sets or checks the water walking flag.
*   **SetHover / SetHoverReal / IsHovering**: Sets or checks the hover flag.
*   **SetFeatherFall / SetFeatherFallReal / IsFallingSlow**: Sets or checks the feather fall flag.
*   **SetLevitate**: Sets the levitate flag.
*   **SetFly**: Sets the fly flag.
*   **StopMoving**: Interrupts current movement splines.
*   **NearTeleportTo / TeleportPositionRelocation / NearLandTo**: Moves the unit instantly to a new location, handling transport offsets and visibility updates.
*   **MonsterMove / MonsterMoveWithSpeed**: Initiates a spline movement to a specific point.
*   **ExtrapolateMovement**: Predicts the unit's future position based on current movement flags and speed, used for line-of-sight checks and anti-cheat.
*   **CheckPendingMovementChanges / ResolvePendingMovementChanges / ResolvePendingMovementChange / PopPendingMovementChange / PushPendingMovementChange / HasPendingMovementChange / HasPendingMovementChange#2 / FindPendingMovementFlagChange / FindPendingMovementRootChange / FindPendingMovementTeleportChange / FindPendingMovementKnockbackChange / FindPendingMovementSpeedChange**: Part of the anti-cheat system. Ensures the client acknowledges server-side movement changes (speed, root, teleport). If the client fails to acknowledge, the server enforces the change.
*   **CanReachWithMeleeAutoAttack / CanReachWithMeleeAutoAttackAtPosition**: Checks if the unit is within melee range of a target, accounting for combat reach and Z-axis limits.
*   **GetMeleeReach / GetCombatReach / GetCombatReach#2 / GetCombatReachToTarget**: Retrieves combat reach values.
*   **SetMeleeZLimit / GetMeleeZLimit / SetMeleeZReach / GetMeleeZReach**: Manages Z-axis limits for melee attacks.
*   **GetRandomAttackPoint**: Calculates a random attack point around the unit.
*   **IsStopped**: Checks if the unit is stopped.
*   **DisableSpline**: Disables the current movement spline.
*   **SetWalk**: Sets the walk flag.
*   **GetMinChaseDistance / GetMaxChaseDistance / HasDistanceCasterMovement / SetCasterChaseDistance**: Manages chase distances for casters.
*   **RestoreMovement**: Restores the unit's movement generator after interruption.
*   **SetInFront / SetFacingTo / SetFacingToObject / IsBehindTarget / CantPathToVictim / IsInAccessablePlaceFor**: Orientation and pathing helpers.
*   **CanWalk / CanFly / CanSwim**: Declarations for checking inhabit types.
*   **IsInWater / IsUnderwater / CanSwimAtPosition**: Checks terrain conditions.
*   **GetSpeedForMovementInfo / ExtrapolateMovement**: Helpers for movement calculation.
*   **SetJumpInitialSpeed / GetJumpInitialSpeed**: Manages jump initial speed.
*   **GetMovementCounterAndInc / GetMovementCounter / GetLastCounterForMovementChangeType**: Manages movement counters for anti-cheat.
*   **HasPendingSplineDone / SetSplineDonePending**: Manages spline completion status.
*   **KnockBack / KnockBackFrom**: Applies knockback effects.
*   **IsMovedByPlayer / GetPlayerMovingMe**: Checks if the unit is being moved by a player (e.g., via possession).
*   **SendHeartBeat / SendMovementPacket**: Sends low-level movement packets to the client.
*   **UpdateSplineMovement**: Updates the unit's position based on the current spline.
*   **HandleInterruptsOnMovement**: Interrupts spells and auras when the unit moves.
*   **OnRelocated / ProcessRelocationVisibilityUpdates**: Handles visibility updates after relocation.
*   **SetSpeedRateHelper / operator()#4**: Helper functor for setting speed rates on controlled units.
*   **PropagateSpeedChange**: Propagates speed changes to controlled units.

### Threat and Aggro
*   **GetThreatManager / GetThreatManager#2 / GetHostileRefManager / GetHostileRefManager#2**: Accesses the managers for incoming threat (who threatens this unit) and outgoing threat (who this unit threatens).
*   **AddThreat / DeleteThreatList / DoResetThreat / RemoveAttackersThreat**: Adds threat to a target, clears the threat list, resets threat, or removes threat from attackers.
*   **SelectHostileTarget**: Chooses the next target to attack based on the threat list. If no target is found, it may trigger evade mode.
*   **TauntApply / TauntFadeOut / AddTauntCaster / RemoveTauntCaster / GetTauntTarget**: Manages taunt effects, forcing the unit to attack the taunter.
*   **IsInCombat / SetInCombatWith / SetInCombatWithAggressor / SetInCombatWithVictim / SetInCombatWithAssisted / SetInCombatState / ClearInCombat / SetOutOfCombatWithAggressor / SetOutOfCombatWithVictim**: Manages the combat state flag and timers. Entering combat often interrupts non-combat spells and triggers AI reactions.
*   **CombatStop / CombatStopWithPets / CombatStopInRange / StopAttackFaction**: Exits combat, interrupts spells, and clears threat/hostile references.
*   **OnEnterCombat / OnLeaveCombat**: Virtual hooks for entering or leaving combat.
*   **UsesPvPCombatTimer**: Checks if the unit uses the 5-second PvP combat timer.
*   **GetCombatTimer / SetCombatTimer**: Manages the combat timer.
*   **TogglePlayerPvPFlagOnAttackVictim**: Toggles the PvP flag on the victim when attacked.
*   **CanHaveThreatList**: Checks if the unit can have a threat list.
*   **IsSecondaryThreatTarget**: Checks if the unit is a secondary threat target (e.g., totems).
*   **ApplyTotalThreatModifier**: Applies a modifier to threat.
*   **InterruptSpellsCastedOnMe / InterruptAttacksOnMe**: Interrupts spells or attacks cast on the unit.
*   **CombatStopWithPetsHelper / operator()**: Helper functor for stopping combat with pets.
*   **StopAttackFactionHelper / operator()#5**: Helper functor for stopping attacks on a faction.
*   **SetPvPHelper / operator()#3**: Helper functor for setting PvP state.
*   **ThreatTransferDo / operator()#6**: Helper functor for transferring threat.

### Relations and Ownership
*   **GetOwner / GetOwnerGuid / SetOwnerGuid / GetOwnerCreature / GetOwnerPlayer / GetOwnerPlayerOrPlayerItself**: Manages the owner of a summoned unit (e.g., a pet's owner).
*   **GetCharmer / GetCharmerGuid / SetCharmerGuid / GetCharmerOrOwner / GetCharmerOrOwnerGuid / GetCharmerOrOwnerOrOwnGuid / GetCharmerOrOwnerOrSelf / GetCharmerOrOwnerOrSelf#2 / GetCharmerOrOwnerPlayer / GetCharmerOrOwnerPlayerOrPlayerItself / IsCharmerOrOwnerPlayerOrPlayerItself**: Manages the charmer of a charmed unit.
*   **GetPet / SetPet / GetPetGuid / SetPetGuid / UnsummonOldPetBeforeNewSummon / GetMiniPet**: Manages the primary pet of a player or creature.
*   **GetCharm / SetCharm / Uncharm / GetCharmGuid / SetCharmGuid / RemoveCharmAuras / RemoveSummonPossessedAuras**: Manages a charmed unit controlled by this unit.
*   **GetGuardian / AddGuardian / RemoveGuardian / RemoveGuardians / RemoveGuardiansWithEntry / FindGuardianWithEntry / GetGuardianCountWithEntry / GetGuardiansCount**: Manages guardian pets (e.g., Warlock demons, Shaman totems).
*   **GetTotem / _AddTotem / _RemoveTotem / GetTotemGuid / IsAllTotemSlotsUsed / UnsummonAllTotems / _GetTotem**: Manages
